# Morshidi — Codex Progress Record

## Course Offerings Opaque Autocomplete & Layering Fix (2026-10-27; completed and verified locally)

**Phase/slice, objective, and starting state:** Fixed visual defects in the Course Offerings (`/student/offerings`) autocomplete dropdown and section cards on branch `release/university-ready-20261024` at starting HEAD `736d9bcbccd61ec65cc25bfea96dfaf8e8ec6b5a`. The primary defects were dropdown translucency (underlying course cards and text showing through the dropdown), text collisions between long course titles and badges, lack of stacking context isolation, and missing `--color-card` mapping in Tailwind CSS v4 theme.

**Implementation details:**
1. *Solid Opaque Surface & Stacking Context (`apps/web/src/app/globals.css`, `offerings/page.tsx`):*
   - Root cause of transparency: In `apps/web/src/app/globals.css`, `@theme inline` defined `--color-surface` and `--color-background`, but `--color-card` and `--card` were missing. The class `bg-card` had no background color definition, rendering transparently.
   - Added `--card: var(--bg-surface-raised)` and `--card-foreground: var(--text-primary)` in `:root`.
   - Added `--color-card: var(--card)` and `--color-card-foreground: var(--card-foreground)` in `@theme inline`.
   - In `apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/offerings/page.tsx`, added `relative z-30 isolate` to the combobox wrapper to establish a dedicated stacking context.
   - Styled the autocomplete dropdown listbox (`#offering-course-options`) with explicit solid `#141414` background (`style={{ backgroundColor: "#141414", opacity: 1 }}`), `z-50`, `start-0 end-0`, `rounded-2xl`, border `border-border-strong/40`, and deep shadow `shadow-2xl shadow-black/95`.
   - Added solid `#121212` backgrounds to `SectionCard` and offered course articles, guaranteeing zero transparency bleed-through.
2. *Suggestion Layout, Typography & Badge Alignment (`offerings/page.tsx`):*
   - Redesigned suggestion item layout into a clean, spacious two-tier hierarchy:
     - Top line: Prominent localized course title in bold font (`text-sm font-semibold text-foreground`), paired with canonical code in a discrete monospace pill (`<bdi>{row.course_code}</bdi>`).
     - Subtitle: Subtle secondary language title (e.g. English title in Arabic mode, Arabic title in English mode) with truncation.
     - Status badges: Pinned to the end with fixed flex alignment (`pt-0.5`), preventing badges from wrapping or squeezing course names on mobile/narrow viewports.
   - Hover and active states: `bg-white/[0.1]` for keyboard-selected options, `hover:bg-white/[0.05]` on mouseover, and gold checkmark for current course.
3. *Automated Verification (`offerings/page.test.tsx`):*
   - Added automated tests verifying that the dropdown listbox has a solid opaque surface (`background-color: rgb(20, 20, 20)`), high z-index (`z-50`), and that title, code, and badges render cleanly without colliding.

**Test results and verification metrics:**
- *Offerings Suite:* 3 test files / 47 tests passed in 3.52s (`npm --prefix apps/web test -- "src/app/(student)/student/offerings"`).
- *TypeScript:* `tsc --noEmit` passed with 0 errors.
- *Git Diff Check:* `git diff --check` passed with 0 errors.

**Files changed and verified from disk:**
- `apps/web/src/app/globals.css` (67,233 bytes)
- `apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/offerings/page.tsx` (38,433 bytes)
- `apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/offerings/page.test.tsx` (14,037 bytes)
- `docs/CODEX_PROGRESS.md` (updated)

**Acceptance and next steps:** Autocomplete dropdown is now completely solid and opaque, stacking context is isolated, suggestion typography and badge alignments are clean and responsive. Ready to commit to `release/university-ready-20261024` and diagnose production deployment delivery.

## Premium Arabic Course Offerings UI Polish & Schedule Formatting (2026-10-27; completed and verified locally)

**Phase/slice, objective, and starting state:** Implemented Arabic-first UI polish and natural schedule formatting for the Course Offerings (`/student/offerings`) page on branch `release/university-ready-20261024` at starting HEAD `8181c6d5761a78c8c0afc54f8559a9187c567f87`. The objective was to replace all raw machine enums (`ELIGIBLE`, `SECTION_OPEN`, `KNOWN_OPEN`, `KNOWN_FULL`, `UNKNOWN`, `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, `SNAPSHOT_STALE`), raw ISO weekday numbers (1-7), raw seconds (`11:00:00`), raw timezone identifiers (`Asia/Amman`), and technical debug dumps (`p10-synthetic-v1`, `2027-09-01`) with natural, elegant Arabic typography, status badges, and human-readable 12-hour AM/PM schedules with LTR-isolated numerical times. Preserved all prior AI transformation, course-name autocomplete, and deterministic academic decision engines.

**Implementation details:**
1. *Typed Schedule & Status Localization Formatter (`apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/offerings/offerings-formatting.ts`):*
   - Weekday mapping: Follows the system-verified ISO weekday convention (1=الاثنين, 2=الثلاثاء, 3=الأربعاء, 4=الخميس, 5=الجمعة, 6=السبت, 7=الأحد; English: Monday–Sunday) with graceful fallback for unspecified/out-of-range days.
   - 12-Hour AM/PM conversion: Strips seconds from `HH:mm:ss`, converts 24h to 12h, handles midnight (00:00 -> 12:00 ص) and noon (12:00 -> 12:00 م), and applies localized Arabic `ص`/`م` or English `AM`/`PM` markers.
   - Schedule range formatting: Produces clean spans like `الأحد · 11:00 ص – 12:00 م` and `الثلاثاء · 11:00 ص – 12:30 م`.
   - Typed status mappings: Maps academic decisions (`ELIGIBLE` -> `مؤهل أكاديمياً`, `NOT_ELIGIBLE` -> `غير مؤهل أكاديمياً`), capacity states (`KNOWN_OPEN`/`SECTION_OPEN` -> `مقاعد متاحة`, `KNOWN_FULL`/`SECTION_FULL` -> `الشعبة ممتلئة`), operational states (`AVAILABLE` -> `متاحة للتسجيل`, `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` -> `بيانات الجامعة غير متاحة حالياً`, `SNAPSHOT_STALE` -> `البيانات بحاجة لتحديث`, `OFFERING_COVERAGE_INCOMPLETE` -> `بيانات الفصل غير مكتملة`), modalities (`IN_PERSON` -> `وجاهي`, `ONLINE` -> `عن بُعد`, `HYBRID` -> `مدمج`), planner overlay statuses (`CONFIRMED_KNOWN_OPEN_NONCONFLICTING_SET` -> `جدول مؤكد بدون تعارض`, `NO_CONFIRMED_SET_OR_INCOMPLETE_DATA` -> `تعذّر تأكيد الجدول أو البيانات غير مكتملة`), and conflict reasons (`TIME_OVERLAP` -> `تعارض في أوقات المحاضرات`, `LOCATION_TRAVEL` -> `تعارض في موقع الحرم / القاعة`).
2. *Redesigned Section Card (`apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/offerings/page.tsx`):*
   - Replaced raw text dump with a premium card layout featuring subtle status pills, section ID badge in monospace, and modality pill.
   - Renders each meeting time with clock icon, weekday name, and `<bdi dir="ltr">` wrapper preventing bidirectional punctuation flipping.
   - Displays campus/location cleanly with map pin icon (`t.locationUnspecified` fallback when absent).
   - Formats seat capacity and enrollment counts with users icon (`10 / 30 مقعد مسجل`, `20 متاح`).
3. *Polished Course Details & Planner Overlay (`apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/offerings/page.tsx`):*
   - Replaced raw `<p>{t.academic}: {result.academic_decision} · {t.operational}: {result.operational_state}</p>` and raw debug timestamp string `<p>{result.source_version} · {result.fresh_until}</p>` with subtle, accessible status pill badges.
   - Replaced raw conflict reason strings (`TIME_OVERLAP: SYN-CS101-A / SYN-MATH101-A · 1 · 10:00–11:00 (Asia/Amman)`) with formatted Arabic conflict cards (`تعارض في أوقات المحاضرات: SYN-CS101-A مع SYN-MATH101-A · الاثنين · 10:00 ص – 11:00 ص`).
4. *Comprehensive Verification & Test Suite (`page.test.tsx`, `offerings-formatting.test.ts`):*
   - Added unit test suite for all schedule and status formatters covering ISO weekdays 1-7, 12h time conversions, noon/midnight, time ranges, and enum mappings.
   - Updated existing integration tests to assert on localized Arabic statuses and conflict descriptions.
   - Added negative assertions verifying that raw technical strings (`ELIGIBLE`, `KNOWN_OPEN`, `IN_PERSON`, `Asia/Amman`, `p10-synthetic-v1`) are never exposed to students.

**Test results and verification metrics:**

- *Full Web Suite:* 45 test files / 297 tests passed in 25.85s (`npm --prefix apps/web test`).

- *ESLint:* `npm --prefix apps/web run lint` passed with 0 errors.

## Universal University Integration Engine (2026-10-27; completed and verified locally)

**Phase/slice, objective, and starting state:** Implemented the Universal University Integration Engine on branch `release/university-ready-20261024` at starting HEAD `6e4e34865898dbdc4b8af95d3161f3a1bcc3a787`. Objective was to make Morshidi completely university-agnostic: eliminate hardcoded single-institution assumptions (e.g. single GPA scale, single grading policy, "ZU" institution prefix) and establish a universal institutional architecture with configurable grading policies, course identity and terminology alias resolution, capability discovery, graceful degradation, extensible unknown-field preservation, onboarding diagnostics, and academic AI institutional grounding. Preserved all prior AI transformation, security, and student offering autocomplete work.

**Implementation details:**
1. *Universal Institutional Data Models & Database Migration (`supabase/migrations/20261027120000_universal_university_engine.sql`):*
   - Added `public.university_grading_policies`: stores versioned institutional grading policies with configurable scales (`PERCENTAGE_100`, `GPA_4_0`, `GPA_4_33`, `GPA_5_0`, `LETTER_GRADE`), min/max bounds, passing thresholds, warning thresholds, honors thresholds, and letter grade mappings.
   - Added `public.course_aliases`: indexes university-scoped course aliases, common names, regional variants, and historical codes for discovery without altering canonical course records.
   - Added `public.university_campuses` and `public.university_classrooms`: canonical representations for multi-campus, building, room type, and capacity management.
   - Extended `public.university_mappings` constraint to support `'COURSE_ALIAS'`.
   - Enabled RLS across all tables and applied idempotently to local Supabase database.
2. *Configurable Institutional Grading & GPA Policy Engine (`apps/api/app/university_sync/grading_policy.py`, `models.py`, `adaptive.py`):*
   - Replaced single-policy assumption with `InstitutionalGradingPolicy` and factory supporting `FAKE_UNIVERSITY_GRADING_POLICY_V1`, `UNIVERSAL_PERCENTAGE_100_V1`, `UNIVERSAL_GPA_4_V1`, `UNIVERSAL_GPA_433_V1`, `UNIVERSAL_GPA_5_V1`, and `UNIVERSAL_LETTER_V1`.
   - Formatted and parsed durable policy references: `university:<uuid>:grading-policy:<version>` for all valid versions.
   - Provided deterministic evaluation methods: `is_grade_passing()`, `is_gpa_on_warning()`, `is_gpa_honors()`, and `normalize_to_percentage()`.
   - Maintained 100% backward compatibility for existing Fake University grading policy tests.
3. *Universal Course Identity & Terminology/Alias Resolution (`apps/api/app/catalog/course_identity.py`, `display.py`):*
   - Built `CourseAliasResolver` with Arabic text normalization, diacritic stripping, alef/ta-marbuta relaxation, and Arabic-Indic numeral translation.
   - Implemented strict architectural separation: search/terminology aliases aid discovery and autocomplete, but authoritative institutional course equivalencies (`public.course_equivalencies`) alone determine credit transfer, prerequisite satisfaction, and degree audits (`AI explains — deterministic rules decide`).
4. *Extensible Unknown-Data Handling (`models.py`, `sis_integration/models.py`):*
   - Added `extension_attributes: dict[str, Any]` across student profiles, courses, enrollments, and grades to safely capture unexpected upstream SIS fields without schema breakages or unauthorized privilege escalation.
5. *Capability Discovery & Graceful Degradation (`apps/api/app/sis_integration/capabilities.py`, `models.py`, `mapping_layer.py`):*
   - Standardized capabilities: `CATALOG_READ`, `SECTIONS_READ`, `SCHEDULES_READ`, `CLASSROOMS_READ`, `INSTRUCTORS_READ`, `SEAT_AVAILABILITY_READ`, `REGISTRATION_READ`, `REGISTRATION_WRITE`, `DROP_WRITE`, `REGISTRATION_DROP`, `TRANSCRIPT_READ`, `POLICIES_READ`, `ACADEMIC_CALENDAR_READ`.
   - Enhanced `CapabilityNegotiator.evaluate_feature_degradation()` to map declared capabilities into active vs degraded operational states (`FULL_MUTATION`, `PREVIEW_ONLY`, `ADVISORY_READ_ONLY`).
6. *University Onboarding Specification & Diagnostics Engine (`apps/api/app/sis_integration/onboarding.py`, `routes/institutional_integrations.py`):*
   - Built `UniversityOnboardingSpec` and `OnboardingDiagnosticsEngine` providing preflight validation, SSRF checks, capability matrix audit, mapping coverage checks, and live readiness scorecard.
   - Exposed `POST /api/v1/institutional/integrations/onboarding/validate` and `GET /api/v1/institutional/integrations/onboarding/diagnostics`.
7. *Offerings Autocomplete & AI Grounding (`course-search.ts`, `use-course-identities.ts`, `conversation_agent.py`):*
   - Integrated course alias search scoring in autocomplete (`scoreTextField` on aliases with 0.96 relevance boost).
   - Injected `InstitutionalContext` into `build_conversation_system_instruction` with tenant name, grading scale description, and strict isolation invariant.

## Smart Course Offerings Search & Arabic-First Autocomplete Upgrade (2026-10-27; completed and verified locally)

**Phase/slice, objective, and starting state:** Implemented the focused course offerings search and autocomplete upgrade on branch `release/university-ready-20261024` at unchanged HEAD `6e4e34865898dbdc4b8af95d3161f3a1bcc3a787`. Objective was to replace course-code-based searching with an intelligent, fast, Arabic-first course-name autocomplete experience in the Course Offerings (`/student/offerings`) page. Preserved all prior AI transformation and security work.

**Implementation details:**
1. *Intelligent Arabic Search & Ranking Engine (`apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/offerings/course-search.ts`):*
   - Normalization: Strips tashkeel diacritics, tatweel/kashida, unifies Alef variants (`أ/إ/آ/ٱ` -> `ا`), Alif Maqsura (`ى` -> `ي`), and collapses whitespace while preserving strict ة vs ه distinction for backward compatibility (`normalizeCourseSearch`).
   - Relaxed dialect matching: Maps `ة` to `ه` (`normalizeRelaxedSearch`) so casual/dialect typing (e.g. "تربيه") seamlessly matches catalog names ("التربية الوطنية").
   - Definite article handling: Supports queries with and without Arabic "ال" (`stripArabicArticle`) so "ذكاء" matches "الذكاء الاصطناعي" and "وطنية" matches "التربية الوطنية".
   - 5-Tier Relevance Ranking:
     - Priority 1: Exact normalized course-name match (score 10000).
     - Priority 2: Course name starts with query (score ~8000).
     - Priority 3: A word in course name starts with query (score ~6000).
     - Priority 4: Partial substring match within course name (score ~4000).
     - Priority 5: Course code or alias match (score ~3000).
   - Single-character instant matching (e.g. "ت" or "C").
   - Case-insensitive English matching (e.g. "machine" -> "Machine Learning").
   - Semester offering relevance boost: courses offered in the active semester receive a +25 score bonus, prioritizing enrollable courses while keeping all catalog courses accessible.
   - Deduplication by canonical course code.
2. *Live Autocomplete Dropdown & Accessible UI (`apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/offerings/page.tsx`):*
   - Replaced raw unranked filter with `rankCourseSuggestions()`.
   - Dropdown item displays full course name as bold primary label, canonical course code in secondary LTR text, and distinct status badges ("مطروحة هذا الفصل" vs "في الخطة الدراسية").
   - Clicking a suggestion selects the course immediately and fills the input with the full localized course name.
   - Preserves canonical `course_code` internally for dependent academic checks (`api.student(row.course_code, period)`).
   - Added clear button (X) when query is populated, resetting search and selection.
   - Accessible keyboard navigation: ArrowUp/ArrowDown, Enter to select, Escape to dismiss, `scrollIntoView` for active options, and outside-click container dismissal.
   - Grid of semester courses filters and ranks offered courses intelligently matching the query.
3. *Inspection of University Admin Offering Flow:*
   - Existing architecture already defines `CourseOfferingProvider` interface with two implementations: `FakeOfferingProvider` (sandbox/synthetic testing) and `AuthenticatedUniversityOfferingProvider` (`apps/api/app/offerings/authenticated_university_provider.py`).
   - `AuthenticatedUniversityOfferingProvider` connects to university systems via `RegistrationService` / `UniversitySyncClient`.
   - No separate university repository is needed for the student offerings search upgrade; the upstream university API envelope and client are already integrated.

**Test results and observed corrections:**
- *Course Search Unit Tests:* 15/15 passed in 19ms (`apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/offerings/course-search.test.ts`).
- *Offerings Page Component Tests:* 13/13 passed in 888ms (`apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/offerings/page.test.tsx`).
- *Total Frontend Test Suite:* 44 test files / 279 tests passed in 25.56s (`npm --prefix apps/web test`).
- *TypeScript:* `tsc --noEmit` passed with 0 errors.
- *Lint:* full ESLint passed with 0 errors (65 pre-existing warnings).
- *Diff check:* `git diff --check` passed with 0 errors.

## Morshidi AI — RAG Verification, Local Supabase RPC Security, Multi-Turn Intelligence, and Frontend Card Persistence (2026-10-27; completed and verified locally)

**Phase/slice, objective, and starting state:** Continued the interrupted Morshidi AI academic intelligence transformation on branch `release/university-ready-20261024` at starting HEAD `6e4e34865898dbdc4b8af95d3161f3a1bcc3a787`. All prior Codex changes and untracked files were recovered and preserved without reset or overwrite. The objective was to complete and verify the interrupted RAG work (intent classification, Arabic normalization, live local Supabase RPC security, tenant isolation, hybrid search with reciprocal rank fusion, explicit lexical fallback, semantic fail-closed behavior), expand multi-turn advisor routing (option comparisons, What-If context tracking, plan impact follow-ups, compound multi-tool planning), and complete frontend structured card persistence across thread switches and reloads.

**Implementation and security details:**
1. *Institutional Policy RAG & Query Intelligence (`apps/api/app/institutional_policy/query.py` & `service.py`):* Expanded policy query planning to recognize five critical academic sub-regulations (`academic_standing`, `course_load`, `postponement`, `incomplete`, and `course_repeat`) alongside existing categories (`withdrawal`, `attendance`, `examinations`, `grades`, `registration`, `graduation`, `transfer`, `discipline`, `fees`). Enhanced Arabic query matching to handle Teh-Marbuta (`ة`) vs Heh (`ه`) spelling variants seamlessly while strictly preserving original strings. Re-ordered concept matching so specific sub-regulations evaluate before broad umbrella terms. Hardened hybrid score sorting in `_fuse_policy_results` with safe dictionary key fallbacks.
2. *Advisor Tool Executor Bug Fix (`apps/api/app/advisor/tool_executor.py`):* Fixed an unhandled fallthrough bug in `get_current_enrollments` where its `return ExecutedTool(...)` was mistakenly pasted under `run_academic_what_if`.
3. *Multi-Turn & Multi-Tool Routing (`apps/api/app/advisor/pre_router.py`):* Extended `_has_pronoun_reference` and context-aware pre-routing to handle follow-up queries: option comparison ("طيب قارنلي الحلين" -> `OPTION_COMPARISON`), What-If follow-up with context course ("وإذا رسبت فيها؟" -> `ACADEMIC_WHAT_IF_SIMULATION`), plan impact follow-up ("شو رح يتأثر بخطتي؟" -> `DEGREE_PATH_PLANNING`), and compound multi-tool planning ("شوف شو ضايل علي مواد، واعطيني أفضل جدول للفصل الجاي، وقارنلي إذا بقدر أتخرج خلال سنة.").
4. *Live Local Supabase RPC & Security Verification (`apps/api/tests/test_policy_rag_live_local_supabase.py`):* Built a comprehensive live integration suite targeting the real local Supabase stack (`http://127.0.0.1:54321`) running on Docker Desktop. Tested and verified with real Postgres RPCs: (a) strict university tenant isolation (`p_university_id`), (b) resistance to SQL injection and system prompt injection attempts, (c) category and document-scope filtering, (d) exclusion of unverified, draft, and superseded passages, (e) denial of direct RPC execution to anon and student roles (401/403/404), (f) hybrid retrieval with explicit `lexical_fallback` and `fallback_reason="policy.embedding.not_configured"`, (g) strict semantic-only fail-closed behavior (`PolicyEmbeddingError`), and (h) grounded citation generation referencing real database passage IDs.
5. *Frontend Structured Card Persistence (`apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/advisor/page.tsx`):* Implemented client-side session caching for validated structured advisor cards (`AdvisorResultCard`) using `window.sessionStorage` keyed by message ID (`morshidi:advisor_card:{messageId}`). When reloading conversations via `getConversationMessages()`, cached structured cards are automatically restored for assistant messages, ensuring rich visualizations (progress, eligibility, semester plans, degree paths, recommendations, comparisons) remain intact across page reloads and thread switching without database schema migrations. Added busy/loading accessibility states.

**Test results and observed corrections:**
- *Policy Query Intelligence:* 66/66 passed in 0.38s (`apps/api/tests/test_policy_query_intelligence.py`).
- *Live Local Supabase Security Suite:* 7/7 passed in 8.72s (`apps/api/tests/test_policy_rag_live_local_supabase.py`).
- *Academic AI Routing Evaluation:* 66/66 passed in 0.44s (`apps/api/tests/test_academic_ai_routing_evaluation.py`).
- *Advisor Pre-Router:* 32/32 passed in 0.32s (`apps/api/tests/test_advisor_pre_router.py`).
- *Core Conversation Tests:* 14/14 passed in 3.10s (`apps/api/tests/test_p156_conversations.py`).
- *Total Backend Focused Gate:* 185 tests collected, 178 passed (offline) / 7 passed (live local Supabase) in 8.72s. Zero failures.
- *Frontend Suite:* 43 test files / 261 tests passed in 25.8s (`npm --prefix apps/web test`).
- *Advisor Persistence Test:* 32/32 tests passed in `portal-pages.test.tsx` including the new card restoration test.
- *TypeScript & ESLint:* `tsc --noEmit` passed with 0 errors; full ESLint passed with 0 errors (63 pre-existing warnings).
- *Diff Check:* `git diff --check` passed with 0 errors.

**Security and architectural invariants verified:**
- AI explains — deterministic rules decide. All academic eligibility, graduation checks, and plan rules strictly flow through existing deterministic engines.
- Strict tenant isolation: Postgres RPCs enforce `d.university_id = p_university_id`. Malicious prompts and role manipulation cannot expand tenant or document boundaries.
- Direct RPC access is denied to untrusted roles (anon, authenticated students); only service_role backend execution is permitted.
- Semantic-only search fails closed when vector infrastructure is unavailable; hybrid search falls back to explicitly labeled `lexical_fallback`.
- No credentials, tokens, or private data logged or exposed. No production resources touched. No destructive git commands executed.

## Academic AI context, routing evaluation, and structured chat results (2026-10-26; validation in progress)

**Phase/slice, objective, and starting state:** Began the smallest coherent local slice of the requested Academic AI transformation on `release/university-ready-20261024` at unchanged starting HEAD `6e4e34865898dbdc4b8af95d3161f3a1bcc3a787`. Re-read this record, reconciled the existing dirty worktree, inspected the root/frontend instructions, roadmap, advisor/provider/tool/orchestration/conversation/RAG/academic-engine boundaries and chat UI/tests, and preserved all prior modified/untracked work. Scope is limited to deterministic intent/context quality, a fixed evaluation matrix, and truthful structured rendering of existing advisor results. No academic rule, authorization, database/RLS/migration, provider credential/model/cost setting, Production resource, backup/OAuth resource, registration record, stage, commit, push, merge, or deployment is changed.

**Baseline and findings:** The existing system already has a bounded Responses API tool loop, strict owner-injected read-only tool contracts, deterministic eligibility/progress/recommendation/semester/degree engines, governed policy retrieval, Student Intelligence, owner/tenant-scoped conversation persistence, and guarded explanation generation. The focused existing advisor/conversation baseline passed `197` tests with two dependency deprecation warnings. Code inspection found a concrete context defect: the 1,600-character bound retained the oldest of 20 turns and could discard the newest question/answer pair. A new fixed 61-case routing evaluation then measured `56/61`; failures were English GPA, academic-record, next-semester recommendation, eligible-to-enroll, and course-comparison forms. The current frontend rendered explanation prose and authority but discarded the already-returned structured result visually.

**Implementation so far:** Conversation context now caps user-stated preferences and summary independently, retains the newest complete turns within 1,600 characters, and returns retained turns chronologically. The deterministic pre-router now resolves canonical course codes for `هاي المادة` / `this course` references, handles the Jordanian `النظام مش سامحلي` eligibility form, safely reuses only the newest prior `USER` intent for compact `ليش؟` / `Why is that?` evidence follow-ups, and covers the five failed high-confidence English forms without moving academic decisions into routing. A new 61-case evaluation covers Arabic, English, mixed/Jordanian forms, status, requirements, recommendations, eligibility, course information/comparison, planning, Student Intelligence, contextual pronouns/deictics, short follow-ups, unknown policy, prompt injection, and safe semantic fallback. The web chat now marks its log busy during a request, renders known deterministic payloads as accessible progress/eligibility/recommendation/semester/path/course/comparison cards, displays actual evidence source/version chips, enlarges key targets, stacks dense results on mobile, and disables the loading animation under reduced motion. Unknown payloads remain hidden rather than shown as raw JSON; no fake tool progress was added.

**Test results and observed corrections:** The first context/router run exposed three canonical-reference selection failures caused by assistant-adjacent course names winning over their paired codes; canonical code precedence fixed them. The fixed routing evaluation improved on the same cases from `56/61` to `61/61`; evaluation plus context/router/conversation tests pass `107/107`, and the broader advisor/conversation gate passes `267/267`, both with two dependency warnings. The first UI run exposed one unsupported Vitest DOM matcher; replacing it with a native attribute check produced `10/10` focused chat tests. The complete frontend suite passes `43 files / 260 tests`; TypeScript passes; full ESLint exits zero with `63` pre-existing warnings and no errors; focused changed-file ESLint passes without output; and the Next.js 16.3.5 production build passes with `41` routes. Python `compileall` passes. The first complete backend run produced `2,524 passed, 4 failed, 107 skipped, 6 warnings`; all four failures were loopback connection failures in the unmarked local-Supabase degree-path/free-elective audit while Docker Desktop was stopped. After starting the installed local Docker Desktop and reusing the preserved local Supabase stack without reset or migration, those exact four tests passed `4/4`, and a fresh complete run passed `2,528`, skipped `107` explicitly opt-in integration cases, and emitted six dependency/serializer warnings in 215.86 seconds.

**Security and authority review:** Owner identity and university scope remain injected by the authenticated service/repository boundaries; neither the model nor tool arguments may select a student or tenant. Context remains labeled untrusted and cannot replace live academic state. Short follow-ups reuse only the latest bounded `USER` turn, never assistant prose, and still flow into existing deterministic engines. Course references remain catalog-resolved before decisions. The UI renders a closed set of known result discriminators, caps displayed recommendations, maps evidence sources to presentation labels with a generic safe fallback, and hides unknown payloads instead of dumping internal JSON. No eligibility, prerequisite, graduation, progress, GPA, semester, recommendation, policy, RAG, authorization, tenant, database, migration, model, provider, credential, cost, or Production behavior was weakened or changed. No real student data was introduced. Local Docker/Supabase startup was the only runtime mutation; no local reset or migration ran. Nothing was staged, committed, pushed, merged, deployed, or written to Production.

**Files changed and verified from disk:** `apps/api/app/advisor/pre_router.py` 33,339 bytes; `apps/api/app/student_conversation/context.py` 14,796; `apps/api/tests/test_advisor_pre_router.py` 14,146; `apps/api/tests/test_p156_conversations.py` 33,535; new `apps/api/tests/test_academic_ai_routing_evaluation.py` 8,949; new `apps/web/src/components/academic/AdvisorResultCard.tsx` 5,204; advisor page 15,719; advisor page test 15,255; `apps/web/src/app/experience.css` 13,629; `apps/web/src/lib/labels.ts` 3,246. This progress record was 857,655 bytes before this final completion update. Every listed file exists and is nonzero. Scoped whitespace/diff verification is the final command after this entry; existing unrelated dirty-tree files and line-ending warnings remain preserved.

**Acceptance, limitations, and exact next point:** This coherent slice is **ACCEPTED LOCALLY**: newest-turn context preservation, high-confidence multilingual routing, deictic/follow-up resolution, a fixed 61-case evaluation, deterministic result cards, evidence labels, honest busy state, mobile reflow, target sizing, and reduced-motion handling are implemented and verified. This does not complete the user's full multi-phase transformation: token streaming, persisted structured cards on history reload, provider-backed conversational quality/latency/token benchmarks, RAG retrieval/reranking/citation evaluation, deterministic What-If expansion, verified live offering/schedule optimization, and a full browser-to-local-Supabase synthetic demonstration remain separate roadmap slices. Exact next starting point is a measured RAG/citation baseline using governed synthetic policy fixtures, followed by provider-enabled Arabic multi-turn evaluation with cost/latency capture; do not infer those results from this routing suite, and do not touch Production or provider cost settings without separate review.

## Laptop Phase 3 - local security hardening (2026-10-25)

**Objective/start/scope:** Continued on `release/university-ready-20261024` at starting/current HEAD `6e4e34865898dbdc4b8af95d3161f3a1bcc3a787`. Re-read this record and the frontend instructions, verified the actual filesystem/Git state, and preserved the 15 modified tracked files and 32 untracked files present at entry, including the Phase 2 lock repair. No Git reset/stage/commit/push/merge, production database/API/Storage access, deployment, backup activation, credential validation/rotation, or recovery-key action occurred.

**Backend `.env` hardening:** Inspected only metadata and security descriptors for `apps/api/.env`; no credential value was displayed or sent anywhere. Before hardening it was 216 bytes, owned by `MOHANNAD\mabua`, inherited its ACL, and granted inherited FullControl to Administrators/SYSTEM, read/execute to `BUILTIN\Users`, and modify to `Authenticated Users`. Captured the exact pre-change SDDL in `C:\Users\mabua\AppData\Local\Morshidi\security-rollback\.env-acl-before-20261025-191938.sddl`, inside a newly created ACL-protected rollback directory outside Git. Applied a non-recursive ACL change to this file only: inheritance disabled and discarded; explicit FullControl/Allow entries only for the current account, SYSTEM, and BUILTIN Administrators; owner remains `MOHANNAD\mabua`. Post-change verification found exactly those three SIDs, no ordinary Users/Authenticated Users rule, no inherited rule, and successful current-account file open. File length, SHA-256 comparison, and last-write timestamp remained unchanged. The protected `RESTORE-ENV-ACL.txt` (755 bytes) beside the snapshot records an emergency `SetSecurityDescriptorSddlForm` rollback and warns that restoration reintroduces the former broad access.

**Credential provenance:** Provenance remains unverified. Prior evidence proves this file was created on the laptop after the transfer manifest explicitly excluded the desktop `.env`, but available operation records do not identify the creating actor/process or validate where the credential came from. The key was not used for validation. Backend startup explicitly overrode the advisor key/model with unusable whitespace values, and the university base URL with a loopback-only unreachable endpoint, while using only the running local Supabase URL/server key in process memory.

**Persistent local frontend configuration:** Created ignored `apps/web/.env.local` (182 bytes) without overwriting an existing file. It contains exactly `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`, and `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`. The API is exactly `http://127.0.0.1:8000`; the Supabase URL and publishable key exactly match the currently running local CLI status. Both URLs are HTTP loopback; no secret/service-role, advisor, university, or hosted-production setting is present. `apps/web/.gitignore:34` excludes it and `git ls-files` returns zero. The backend environment remains excluded by root `.gitignore:26` and is also untracked.

**Focused validation:** Supervised FastAPI startup on `127.0.0.1:8012` with the advisor credential disabled returned `/health` HTTP 200 and was stopped. Supervised Next.js 16.3.5 development startup on `127.0.0.1:3012` loaded `.env.local`; `/` and `/login` both returned HTTP 200 and the server was stopped. Local Supabase Auth health returned HTTP 200, its API URL is loopback HTTP, and `pg_isready` reported the `supabase_db_Morshidi` PostgreSQL container accepting connections. No full test/build suite was repeated. An initial combined hidden-process harness was rejected by command policy before execution; direct supervised sessions then passed. One aggregate verification invocation stopped on Supabase CLI's informational stopped-service stderr under strict PowerShell native-error handling; it made no change and the corrected invocation passed.

**Acceptance/exact next point:** Backend secret-file ACL hardening, protected rollback, persistent local frontend configuration, local API/frontend startup, local Supabase connectivity, and Git exclusion PASS. ## Laptop Phase 2 - npm lock repair and local-configuration safety review (2026-10-25)

**Objective/start/scope:** Continued on `release/university-ready-20261024` at starting/current HEAD `6e4e34865898dbdc4b8af95d3161f3a1bcc3a787`. Re-read this record, verified the filesystem and Git state, and preserved the 14 modified tracked files plus 32 untracked development files present at entry. Scope was limited to a reviewed lockfile repair and local-only configuration/security validation. No reset, stage, commit, push, merge, deployment, production database/API/Storage access, release gate, backup activation, credential rotation, or recovery-identity generation occurred.

**Lockfile root cause and repair:** The committed npm v3 lock was missing the root optional peer records `@emnapi/core@1.11.3` and `@emnapi/runtime@1.11.3` required by the locked WASM/native dependency closure, so both npm 10 and npm 11 rejected it as inconsistent. Regeneration experiments using official npm 9.9.4, 10.9.4, and 11.16.0 all produced the same dependency closure. Applied `npm install --package-lock-only --ignore-scripts --no-audit --no-fund` with the installed supported npm 11.16.0. `apps/web/package.json` remained byte-identical at SHA-256 `95497AC62252479C55C085AFF0E631AE34EF5C6B18BD062635219F1800824C9A`; no pre-existing package version, resolved URL, or integrity value changed. The lock repair added eight records: the two missing root `@emnapi` records and six bundled records already belonging to `@tailwindcss/oxide-wasm32-wasi` (`@emnapi/core@1.11.1`, `@emnapi/runtime@1.11.1`, `@emnapi/wasi-threads@1.2.2`, `@napi-rs/wasm-runtime@1.1.4`, `@tybys/wasm-util@0.10.2`, and `tslib@2.8.1`). Official npm also recalculated `peer` classification metadata on 21 existing records. No record was removed and no dependency version changed. Final lock size is 359,928 bytes, SHA-256 `B47222CE835DFA5C3CEA69EF532B2E593AB6EC7442C75ECA950A5DF9BAD04700`; its resolved download host is only `registry.npmjs.org`. Diff before this progress entry: 101 insertions / 21 deletions in the lockfile.

**Clean install and focused verification:** A disposable frontend copy under the current user's Temp directory received only the repaired `package.json` and lock. `npm ci --no-audit --no-fund` succeeded and installed 537 packages in 46 seconds; `npm ls --depth=0` passed. The existing working `node_modules` was not deleted or replaced. In the canonical checkout, `npm exec -- vitest run src/lib/auth-structure.test.ts` passed 3/3 tests, `npm run typecheck` passed, and `npm run build` passed under Next.js 16.3.5 with 41 routes. The only install notices were upstream ESLint deprecation and an npm allow-scripts notice for `unrs-resolver@1.12.2`; neither was an install failure. The previously completed full backend/frontend suites were not repeated.

**Ignored backend environment provenance and access:** `apps/api/.env` is 216 bytes, created locally at `2026-10-25T18:32:40.0782092+03:00` and last written at `2026-10-25T18:34:23.1384366+03:00`. The transfer manifest was created at 17:25 and explicitly says this path was excluded and no real `.env` was present in staging. Therefore this particular file was created on the laptop after transfer, not transferred from the desktop. Available setup records contain inspection but no recorded creation/write action, so the creating actor/process and whether its values were manually re-entered cannot be established. Content was classified without displaying values: `ADVISOR_LLM_API_KEY` is populated and credential-like/non-placeholder; `ADVISOR_LLM_MODEL` is populated. Git ignore resolves through `.gitignore:26`; tracked count and Git-history count are both zero. Owner is `MOHANNAD\mabua`, but the ACL inherits `Administrators` and `SYSTEM` full control, `BUILTIN\Users` read/execute, and `Authenticated Users` modify. It is therefore not owner-only and needs a separately approved ACL hardening action; the file and credentials were not changed.

**Credential-artifact and local-configuration safety:** Compared the populated advisor key value against all 1,037 tracked and non-ignored untracked artifacts without emitting it: zero matches. A high-confidence scan for private-key headers, age identities, GitHub/AWS/OpenAI-style tokens, and Supabase secret-key forms also returned zero matches. The running Supabase project is unlinked, uses only loopback endpoints, has project ID/container suffix `Morshidi`, PostgreSQL image 17.11.0.006, and 32 migration files; this distinguishes it from MORSHIDI Production. The running stack supplies a local publishable key and local server key in process memory, so the three existing `apps/web/.env.example` fields can be populated entirely with local values; no new example file was necessary and no key was written to Git. A process-only local configuration produced frontend configuration completeness/loopback checks and FastAPI `/health` HTTP 200. With advisor credentials explicitly unusable and no university identity/credentials, startup selected `FakeUniversityOfferingProvider`, left registration unconfigured, and made no external provider usable. A deliberately invalid whitespace university identity failed startup validation rather than connecting externally. Focused fail-closed tests passed 3/3: unconfigured production advisor provider, unconfigured real eligibility repository, and university upstream timeout mapping to safe 503 `UNIVERSITY_UNAVAILABLE`; two existing Starlette/httpx deprecation warnings remain.

**Acceptance and exact next point:** Lockfile clean-install reproducibility, focused frontend behavior/build, loopback-only local configuration, and fail-closed optional integrations PASS. Normal local laptop development is ready without production credentials. Remaining local blocker: the ignored advisor `.env` has a real credential-like value and broader inherited access than owner-only; its provenance beyond laptop-side creation is unknown. Separately optional/private capabilities still require their own credentials and the eight deliberately excluded learning-material binaries. The exact next action, only with operator approval, is to restrict the `.env` ACL or replace it with explicitly provisioned local-only configuration; otherwise continue normal development without starting any production release or backup gate.

## Backup activation Phase 2 — no-external-drive custody adaptation (2026-10-25; prepared, encryption state/operator choice pending)

**Scope/start/constraints:** Continued on `release/university-ready-20261024` at starting/current HEAD `6e4e34865898dbdc4b8af95d3161f3a1bcc3a787` after learning that no USB/external disk is available. No production/Supabase access, rclone authorization, upload, task, migration, deployment, or Git stage/commit/push/merge occurred.

**System-drive evidence:** Attempted only status queries for the Windows system volume through `Get-BitLockerVolume -MountPoint C:\` and `manage-bde -status C:`. Both returned access denied/administrative rights required before revealing protection state. No key-protector/recovery-key command or property was requested. Mohannad's actual BitLocker/Windows Device Encryption state is therefore **unverified**, not reported off. The real ceremony now requires an elevated private terminal and stops before key generation if encryption status is inaccessible, protection is off, encryption is incomplete, or the volume is locked.

**Security decision:** One fully encrypted internal system volume per custodian on two separately controlled physical computers is conditionally suitable for the current logical-backup recovery requirement. It is not sufficient alone: each custodian must also complete a computer-independent recovery copy as approved sealed offline paper or an approved end-to-end encrypted credential vault with independent account recovery. Two partitions/drive letters on one physical disk, DPAPI alone, ordinary cloud storage, email, browser notes, or another computer-local-only copy are explicitly rejected.

**Implementation:** `Initialize-MorshidiAgeCustodian.ps1` now supports `SingleEncryptedDevice` and preserved `DualEncryptedVolumes` modes. Single-device mode requires `SealedOfflinePaper` or `ApprovedEncryptedCredentialVault`, an explicit recovery-mechanism-ready switch, the internal system drive, verified full BitLocker/device-encryption protection, a new ACL-restricted/non-synchronized directory, and pinned age-keygen. It emits only public recipient/fingerprint and deliberately reports `LocalIdentityCreatedRecoveryCopyPending`; it cannot attest a paper/vault copy. Dual-volume mode now additionally compares physical disk numbers and refuses two partitions on the same disk. `New-MorshidiCustodianTestArtifact.ps1` now refuses configuration/artifact creation unless both custodian recovery-copy confirmations are explicit.

**Synthetic-only verification:** Added and ran `Test-MorshidiSingleDeviceCustody.ps1`. It inspected the required ceremony guards, generated one disposable X25519 test identity, derived/validated its public fingerprint, encrypted/decrypted 32,768 random synthetic bytes, matched the plaintext hash, removed every test artifact/identity, and reported `ProductionContacted=false`, `RcloneInvoked=false`, and `PrivateIdentityPrinted=false`. Separate fail-closed checks passed for incorrect age-keygen hash, missing two-custodian recovery confirmations, and malformed recipients. All backup PowerShell files parse. Synthetic-test directory is empty; no recipient configuration or custodian artifact exists.

**Documentation:** Replaced the external-drive procedure with the conditional two-computer model and no-drive recovery choices in the primary and Moayad guides. The recommended simplest option is sealed offline paper, subject to operator approval and a private local print/hand-transcription process; an approved independently recoverable encrypted credential vault is the alternative. Updated policy and activation checklist to require elevated encryption evidence, a computer-independent copy per custodian, same-disk-partition rejection, public-only onboarding, two explicit custody confirmations, and two independent synthetic decryptions.

**Files verified from disk:** initializer 11,726 bytes/233 lines, SHA-256 `0FAA324A31AF4FF0004B096D542F61A692BCF09673825F5CDE08B1C8BE73B4ED`; artifact builder 5,952/112, `1E06CC3643C1BEA8FCC7698543BF68BAC88EF00112C152114EBC96BBBBB3FF73`; new synthetic validator 5,107/104, `56D4A900150611C762BAA7FBCE5B326C53FFD0DE7DA71A71D5003B87E563975A`; primary guide 6,973/87; Moayad guide 4,049/57; recovery policy 18,941/145; activation checklist 8,566/83. ## Gate A encrypted Google Drive backup preparation (2026-10-25; prepared, not activated)

**Scope, start, and authority:** Continued the existing Gate A recovery implementation on branch/SHA `release/university-ready-20261024` / `6e4e34865898dbdc4b8af95d3161f3a1bcc3a787`. Reverified the dirty worktree, existing backup scripts/policy/Gate A report, PostgreSQL client, validated CA, and immutable checkpoint before editing. Used the Google Drive and Supabase operating guidance and checked current official Supabase backup guidance/changelog plus official age/rclone release/Drive documentation. No production database connection, dump, Storage operation, migration, deployment, registration/webhook action, Git stage/commit/push/merge, key generation, credential provisioning, or unattended task activation occurred.

**Approved Drive evidence and security design:** The authenticated Google Drive connector can list the owner-approved empty folder `MORSHIDI - Encrypted Backups` (`1bdhhMcT6hHtxjHRu6QlY-8_UQnT3r_q7`); no content was returned and nothing was uploaded. Connector access does not prove the identity or least-privilege boundary of the future unattended account. The implementation therefore requires a dedicated Google account shared only to that folder, a dedicated OAuth client, an encrypted rclone config, a DPAPI-protected config password, and runtime folder-ID pinning. Because rclone `drive.file` cannot see an existing folder created outside rclone, least privilege must come from the dedicated account's folder-only sharing boundary plus the pinned root rather than a false `drive.file` claim.

**Encryption/upload implementation:** Replaced the removable-media completion path with local `age` X25519 authenticated public-key encryption using at least two unique public recipients under separate custody. The private identities are never required for unattended backup. The runner encrypts both the custom PostgreSQL archive and detailed manifest to atomic `.age` artifacts before the uploader can see them. The Drive uploader rejects non-`.age` inputs and unexpected names, uses immutable creates, downloads every uploaded ciphertext, compares SHA-256, and uploads only a safe receipt containing ciphertext metadata and `plaintext_uploaded=false`. It never uploads `.backup`, plaintext manifests, keys, credentials, private Storage bytes, or student data. Drive and local staging retention target only exact timestamped routine patterns older than 30 days after a verified newer run; Drive deletion uses trash behavior. The protected Gate A checkpoint remains 1,776,912 bytes with SHA-256 `D14A70992ED5DB719485A51A54551872CEE15F6D505AE3AEDC0CEF02C6B8FCA0` and was neither changed nor uploaded.

**Custody, tests, alerts, and activation guards:** Added same-account DPAPI initialization for the password of the encrypted rclone configuration; an operator-key encrypted checkpoint round-trip plus isolated restore wrapper; a read-only Drive readiness check; an explicitly guarded random-data encrypted Drive canary; and a sanitized failure-alert marker/command hook. The round-trip script refuses private identities under the repository, Morshidi backup root, or common cloud-sync directories and removes temporary artifacts. The disabled task generator now requires credential, encrypted restore, two-key custody, Drive credential, and alert confirmations in both command switches and active configuration. A new activation checklist defines operators, tool checksum verification, two-custodian key creation, dedicated-account sharing, canary, forced-failure alert, retention, manual disabled-task run, and final enablement approval.

**Verification results:** `age` and `rclone` are absent, so no real encryption, decryption, OAuth/rclone login, Drive write, ciphertext upload/download, or operator-key restore is claimed. All 12 PowerShell scripts/modules parse successfully. `Invoke-MorshidiDatabaseBackup.ps1 -ValidateConfigurationOnly` passed for the current disabled example and truthfully reported `ReadyForUnattendedRun=false`. The offline harness passed checkpoint/archive validation (913 entries; 14 critical tables), log/alert redaction, local 30-day retention, simulated Drive 30-day exact-name retention, failure-alert hook, disabled/password-free task guards, encryption-before-upload ordering, plaintext-upload rejection, and pinned folder identity; `ProductionContacted=false`.

**Files and exact sizes:** common module 25,453 bytes/554 lines; runner 16,023/350; rclone-password initializer 2,123/43; encrypted round-trip test 4,165/87; Drive readiness test 1,957/43; encrypted canary 4,258/80; alert test 723/19; task generator 4,913/113; tooling harness 8,247/160; example config 2,475/63; recovery policy 16,085/140; activation checklist 6,686/73; Gate A report 26,416/297. This progress file was 753,567 bytes/3,227 lines before this entry. All were reread from disk and contain no credential, private key, access token, student record, or private object bytes.

**Acceptance and exact next point:** Encrypted Drive automation is implemented and offline-tested but deliberately inactive. Operator action is required to: install checksum-verified `age` 1.3.2+ and `rclone` 1.75.2+; name two separate key custodians and generate identities offline; establish/attest the dedicated folder-only Google account and encrypted rclone config; run the actual operator-key encrypted round-trip isolated restore; run the guarded encrypted canary; configure and receive the forced-failure alert; provision the database DPAPI credential; choose the schedule/operators; and separately approve one production end-to-end run and later task enablement. Gate A and full Supabase recovery remain blocked; Gate C remains unauthorized.

## University-Ready Gate B offerings provider completion (2026-10-25; locally validated, release authorization blocked)

**Phase/slice, objective, and immutable start:** Continued the latest Gate B reconciliation on `release/university-ready-20261024` at starting/current HEAD `6e4e34865898dbdc4b8af95d3161f3a1bcc3a787`. Reverified the actual branch, HEAD, dirty worktree, existing Gate A/Gate B/recovery/release documents, provider contracts, university identity mapping, registration client/service, upstream Fake University registration endpoints and source manifest, and focused tests before editing. Preserved every pre-existing local Gate A/Storage hardening change. No production write/query, migration, Storage operation, webhook, registration action, credential/configuration change, deployment, stage, commit, push, merge, or Gate C action occurred. Gate A remains blocked.

**Offerings outcome:** Added `AuthenticatedUniversityOfferingProvider` and an owner-scoped provider protocol. Student `/api/v1/me/offerings*` routes now prefer the owner-scoped contract, while institutional consumers retain the old unscoped contract. Production wiring constructs the authenticated adapter only after the Supabase-backed identity repository and `RegistrationService` exist and `UNI_UNIVERSITY_ID` is configured. It resolves authenticated owner to the configured university/student mapping, exchanges one short-lived student token, and concurrently reads current registration context, curriculum-filtered offerings, and the same university origin's source manifest. It introduces no new shared secret and does not use `UNI_SERVICE_KEY`.

**Fail-closed/data semantics:** Exact tenant mismatch makes no upstream call; the authenticated adapter's unscoped methods expose no student data. It accepts only a successful context, initialized manifest, explicit boolean synthetic classification, matching active/current term, nonempty source/institution/version, unique section identities, canonical nonempty course codes, known Arabic/English day/status values, valid times, and internally consistent nonnegative capacity/enrollment/availability. Invalid facts become provider-unavailable without leaking upstream details. A valid empty list is a complete empty snapshot, not an outage. Receipt time supplies a documented five-minute freshness window because upstream exposes no authoritative snapshot timestamp. The current source manifest is explicitly synthetic, so provenance visibly contains `SYNTHETIC`; only an explicit `synthetic=false` manifest can yield `INSTITUTIONAL`. Existing deterministic eligibility/planning and catalog Arabic/English search remain unchanged.

**Configuration/security reconciliation:** Existing evidence still confirms Render service `Morshidi`, `main` auto-deploy, LIVE SHA `b434aadbb8f5839334254728620121ff378d5a18`, all 18 supplied variable names, runtime-reported production environment, and scoped notification caller. Name presence does not verify values, exact MORSHIDI Supabase URL/server key, authorized university base/client/scope, origin allowlist, linked-group precedence, or effective secret counterpart pairing. `UNI_SERVICE_KEY` remains optional legacy/institutional-analytics compatibility only. Webhook/internal secret matching still requires operator verification without firing an event. Pushing/merging `main` remains a production auto-deploy risk.

**Focused verification:** The existing `apps/api/.venv311` resolved the earlier FastAPI dependency blocker without dependency edits. Adjacent suite command `apps\\api\\.venv311\\Scripts\\python.exe -m pytest -q apps/api/tests/test_authenticated_university_offering_provider.py apps/api/tests/test_registration_service.py apps/api/tests/test_registration_api.py apps/api/tests/test_p10_offerings.py apps/api/tests/test_p10_offerings_api.py apps/api/tests/test_university_sync_client_envelopes.py apps/api/tests/test_cors.py` passed **120/120**, with only two dependency deprecation warnings. After tightening initialized-source/status/availability validation, the changed-provider subset passed **29/29** with the same warnings. `py_compile` passed for all changed application modules. `git diff --check` passed with existing LF/CRLF notices only. Ruff is not installed in `.venv311` (`No module named ruff`), so no Ruff result is claimed.

## Phase 8 — real database validation, security hardening, and release readiness (2026-10-23—2026-10-24, complete)

### Phase 8 final validation and release-preparation checkpoint (2026-10-24)

**Outcome:** Phase 8 isolated validation is complete with verdict **`GO FOR RELEASE PREPARATION`**. This does not authorize or claim a production release. Current branch/SHA remain `release/notifications-phase1-4d-20261021` / `b434aadbb8f5839334254728620121ff378d5a18`; nothing was staged, committed, pushed, deployed, or applied to a hosted/production resource. The pre-existing dirty Phase 1-7 worktree and `.phase4d_*` directories were preserved.

**Database, RLS, and durability:** Docker/local Supabase supplied PostgreSQL 17, Auth, PostgREST, and Storage. Both pending migrations passed standalone, combined, fresh-reset, upgrade-preservation, forced-rollback, privilege/RLS, immutability, and real two-session publication tests. The overlapping publication contender waited about 3.3 seconds and was rejected. Upgrade clones preserved 17 plans, 619 courses, 111 groups, and 1,155 memberships and were removed after use. Real synthetic Auth/Storage tests verified AI-only access to all 8 materials, Nursing/unmapped/anonymous denial, cross-tenant and curriculum-version isolation, private direct-object denial, signed-link expiry, server-side authorization, traversal rejection, SHA-256/size integrity, and availability after Storage restart. Final `supabase db reset --local` passed all 31 migrations and left the deliberate pre-deployment state: 8 material rows, 8 authorization rows, private bucket, 0 objects, and no policy fixtures. Production must run the verified object-copy script before switching metadata from `local_private`; bundled files are migration sources, not claimed as the production durability layer.

**Functional acceptance:** Nursing Plan 12 is deterministically 132 credits: 129 accounted plus one explicit 3-credit `FREE_ELECTIVE_POLICY_REQUIRED` category, with no fake course, double count, or lost zero-credit requirement. Offerings now provides tenant periods, initial semester listings, automatic registration-open selection, year/semester selectors, Arabic normalization and first-character search, English/code search, keyboard identity selection, mobile controls, and distinct complete-empty/incomplete/unavailable/stale states. The exact Arabic empty messages were tested. Two fictional universities with overlapping `CS101` were isolated across identities, curricula, totals, calendars, sections, content, policies, and revisions. JUST remains blocked pending authoritative normalized evidence.

**Final verification:** Full backend with the local service settings: **2,537 passed, 1 skipped, 6 warnings in 388.67s**. The sole skip is the explicitly gated legacy Phase 4d notification integration test, not a Phase 8 security or academic-rule test. Phase 8 local Supabase integration: **4/4 passed in 23.14s**. Offerings backend: **36 passed**. Nursing focused: **21 passed**. Degree Path regression: **160 passed**. Privacy regression after typed optional-store outage handling: **24 passed**. API compilation passed. Full frontend: **43 files / 259 tests passed in 80.75s**; TypeScript passed; Next.js production build passed and generated 41 pages. Focused offerings ESLint exited 0 with one `set-state-in-effect` warning. Repository-wide ESLint reports 2 errors and 63 warnings; both errors are unescaped quotes in unchanged `institutional/pilots/[pilot_id]/page.tsx`, so they were classified rather than expanding scope. `git diff --check` passed with line-ending notices only.

**Performance evidence:** The read-only benchmark repeated each case with identical semantic fingerprints and 2 distinct paths. AI end-to-end was 6,418/6,955 ms at 42.8/43.1 MiB; Nursing 4,950/4,856 ms at about 43.2 MiB; synthetic 180-credit/60-course input 4,132/4,014 ms at 43.223 MiB. No before/after improvement is claimed and deterministic logic was not weakened.

**Validation evidence so far:** The first `supabase db reset --local` applied the existing chain through `20261021162616_university_scoped_notifications.sql`, then correctly aborted and rolled back migration `20261022135845_add_learning_material_authorization.sql` with PostgreSQL error `42601`: its authenticated RLS policy used reserved word `authorization` as a relation alias. The alias was narrowly renamed to `material_auth`. The second clean reset passed that point and aborted with `2201B` when its first seed insert evaluated an `object_key` regex containing repetition bound `{0,399}`, which exceeds PostgreSQL's supported bound. That constraint now enforces the same allowlist with `char_length(object_key) <= 400` and an unbounded character-class tail. Both are introduced migration defects, not environment failures; each failed migration rolled back atomically. The complete fresh chain must be rerun before acceptance. No production or hosted resource was read or changed.

**Current acceptance / exact next point:** In progress and **NO-GO**. Rerun a full local reset, inspect the resulting schema/grants/RLS/functions, then exercise real Auth and private Storage access plus isolated upgrade/concurrency scenarios. Remaining backend/frontend failures, Nursing reconciliation, offerings, multi-university isolation, and Degree Path benchmarks are not yet revalidated in this Phase 8 run.

### Phase 8 continuation (2026-10-24)

**Migration and security corrections:** After the two recorded failed resets, a third clean reset applied the complete 31-migration chain and seed successfully. Database review found and corrected two additional release blockers in the still-unreleased graduation-policy migration: `service_role` retained default `TRUNCATE`, `REFERENCES`, and `TRIGGER` privileges because it had not been revoked before narrow grants, and approved policy versions/rules/applicability remained mutable with no overlapping-publication guard. The migration is now transactional, revokes before granting, enforces forward-only `draft -> validated -> approved -> retired` transitions, requires source/applicability/rules before approval, serializes publication per package with a transaction advisory lock, rejects overlapping approved effective ranges, and makes approved/retired versions and governed child content immutable. Trigger functions are not executable by browser roles. The learning-material migration now creates and verifies a 50 MiB MIME-restricted private `learning-materials` bucket without client object policies while retaining `local_private` metadata until a verified object-copy release step.

**Real local Supabase evidence:** Added opt-in integration coverage in `apps/api/tests/test_phase8_local_supabase_integration.py`. Against PostgreSQL 17, GoTrue, PostgREST, and Storage API it verified all nine new tables have RLS, exact grants, eight metadata/authorization rows, lifecycle/immutability/overlap rejection, AI Auth user visibility of exactly eight materials, Nursing and unmapped Auth user visibility of zero, anonymous denial, browser-role graduation-policy denial, direct private-object denial, eight binary size/SHA-256 matches, signed download, expired-link rejection, server-side API authorization (AI redirect; Nursing 404), and availability after restarting the local Storage container. The first real signed-link download exposed that Storage API returns `/object/...` while the repository assumed `/storage/v1/...`; `repository.py` now safely normalizes both supported rooted forms and rejects others. Focused learning-material and integration result: **17 passed in 22.55s**. The test restores all material rows to `local_private`, deletes uploaded test objects/users, and leaves the disposable local database clean of those synthetic identities.

**Backend failure reconciliation:** With local Supabase running, the full suite changed from the checkpoint's `2,406 passed, 19 failed, 103 skipped` to **2,412 passed, 15 failed, 106 skipped** in 221.40s; all four former local-Supabase failures passed and were environmental. The remaining four Advisor failures were a generic-definition pre-router regression: `What is a prerequisite?` and `Explain prerequisites` were treated as a specific-course lookup, bypassing the bounded general-academic explanation path. The remaining eleven CORS failures were a pre-existing incomplete wiring problem: `core/cors.py` expected `cors_allowed_origins`, but `Settings` lacked it and `main.py` still used an independent wildcard-method/header policy. Added the missing setting, wired the validated explicit CORS policy into the application, and narrowed the definition guard without changing deterministic eligibility. Focused Advisor/CORS/pre-router regression: **68 passed, 2 dependency warnings in 2.39s**. A consolidated full rerun remains required.

**Current acceptance / exact next point:** Still **NO-GO** while isolated separate/combined upgrade tests, durable-storage migration tooling, skip audit, Nursing reconciliation, offerings/multi-university acceptance, Degree Path benchmarks, frontend/build checks, and the final consolidated backend run remain outstanding. No hosted or production resource, deployment, Git index, commit, or remote was changed.

File sizes: apps/api/app/main.py: 38750 bytes; apps/api/app/university_sync/event_routes.py: 6036 bytes; apps/api/app/university_sync/events.py: 1862 bytes; apps/api/tests/test_university_sync_contract.py: 5731 bytes; apps/api/tests/test_university_events_activation.py: 7598 bytes. **Security / open problems:** Login uses shadow accounts and authoritative owner/university mapping, not expected email. The frontend polling cannot guarantee delivery of every event. Validate proxy streaming, sender envelope/signature, schema, existing global records, and event version semantics before live testing. Required server configuration includes SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY (or SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY), UNI_UNIVERSITY_ID, UNI_WEBHOOK_SECRET; browser API base and CORS must point to the correct backend.

**Acceptance:** Local backend implementation and focused mocked tests complete; live activation and database security remain unverified. **Exact next starting point:** With separate authorization and safe isolated infrastructure, validate actual event-store migration and RLS/RPC and a signed end-to-end event for the configured single university, then address cursor and isolation limitations as needed. ## Degree Path Multi-Strategy Deduplication — Build Fix & Final Verification (2026-10-21, local implementation complete)

**Objective / slice:** Fix the remaining TypeScript build blocker (`normalFetch` undefined) in `apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/portal-pages.test.tsx` introduced when the 3 new strategy/deduplication tests were added but each called `normalFetch` which was only in-scope in other test closures. Starting SHA: `7c6125e2c83d0c11ee0444f868f7eee1cd53ea1e` (uncommitted working tree). No commit/push.

**Root cause:** Three new `fetchSpy.mockImplementation` callbacks in the added tests (renders distinct strategy tags, renders single candidate fallback, deduplicates duplicate candidate cards) referenced `normalFetch` — a variable that only exists when declared `const normalFetch = fetchSpy.getMockImplementation()!` inside each test's own scope. The new tests omitted that declaration, causing `TS2304: Cannot find name 'normalFetch'` only during `next build` (which type-checks test files), not `tsc --noEmit`.

**Fix applied:**
- In each of the 3 new tests, added a locally scoped capture of the original mock implementation (`normalFetch1`, `normalFetch2`, `normalFetch3`) before calling `fetchSpy.mockImplementation(...)`, and updated the fallback call to `return normalFetchN(input, init)` with the correct `init` parameter.
- File: [`portal-pages.test.tsx`](file:///d:/imporant/Morshidi-review/apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/portal-pages.test.tsx) lines ~939, ~980, ~1041.

**Verification:**
- `npm run build` (Next.js 16.3.5 Turbopack): **exit code 0** — TypeScript clean, 40 static/dynamic routes generated.
- `vitest run portal-pages.test.tsx` (isolated): **31/31 passed in 11.18s** — all 3 new strategy/deduplication tests pass alongside existing 28.
- Full parallel `vitest run` produces a pre-existing environment crash (`Vitest failed to find the current suite` from `globalSetup` context) that affects ALL 43 files identically including unmodified ones (verified by running `labels.test.ts` in isolation: 3/3 pass). This is a pre-existing Vitest parallel-runner/Windows environment issue, not caused by these changes.
- Backend tests: unchanged from previous session (218 passed).
- No academic data, curriculum, student records, grades, GPA, or production resources mutated.
- No git add/commit/push performed.

**Files changed (this session):** `apps/web/src/app/(student)/student/portal-pages.test.tsx` (+3/-2 net).

**Status:** `STATUS = BUILD_BLOCKER_RESOLVED`. Working tree is locally complete with build passing and all new tests green. Pending: explicit user approval to commit and push.

**Exact next starting point:** User approves commit; then `git add -p` + commit with message covering multi-strategy degree path + deduplication + build fix + CODEX update, followed by push to `origin/main`.

## Degree Path candidate identity / curriculum evidence audit (2026-10-21, local implementation complete; academic-data release stop)

**Objective and starting state:** Root-cause and repair the Degree Path selected-option/semester-node mismatch, audit Nursing and all 17 canonical curriculum totals, and review standalone Mock Registration navigation without changing authentication, ownership, routes, P6 replay, or production academic data. Starting branch/SHA: `main` / `1621697e6eb6b846fa7c93baed4b7b6877d39327`; verified clean worktree and matching existing progress claims before edits.

**Root cause confirmed:** the student Degree Path selector is populated from the independent credit-comparison strategy allocator, its fallback/detail metadata is read from `DegreePathResponse.paths` by the same array index, and its explanation graph is fetched through a third call that reruns Degree Path. These objects have no shared candidate identity. `PATH_SUMMARY_SOURCE = credit-comparison scenario`; `PATH_NODE_SOURCE = strategy allocator course_path with index-based fallback to degree-path result`; `PATH_CREDIT_SOURCE = mixed credit timeline, university-reported remaining credits, and degree-path fields`; therefore index alignment can combine unrelated candidates.

**Authoritative Nursing evidence / mandatory stop boundary:** the official Zarqa University Nursing study-plan page at `https://zu.edu.jo/ar/Collage/Nursing/Dept_Nursing/GetStudyPlan.aspx?page=462&fac=08&Dept=0801&id=24` publishes requirement headers of 18, 9, 21, 66, 3, and 12 credits plus a separate 3-credit free-elective requirement, totaling 132. The owner-supplied release artifact `ZU_NURS_NURS_P12.json` contains only the first six groups, 63 memberships, group/canonical total 129, and no free-elective group. Exact discrepancy: omitted 3-credit free-elective requirement; no course will be invented. This requires an academic-data correction and production review. No curriculum artifact, migration, Supabase resource, or production data has been changed. Current safe work is limited to code/UI identity consistency, tests, navigation hiding, and documentation; release remains blocked before any production curriculum mutation.

**Single-source implementation:** each backend candidate now owns a deterministic content-derived `path_id`, exact semester allocation, completed/current/unplaced course classifications, and one canonical credit summary. The fail-closed validator verifies unique/stable IDs; semester totals and unique node courses; disjoint completed/current/placed/unplaced partitions that cover every plan membership; prerequisite order; plan membership; term limits; and `completed + current + future placed + valid unplaced == canonical degree total`. The API DTO/client expose that contract. The explanation graph is built from that same in-memory backend result and embedded in the response, eliminating the former independent graph recomputation request. The student selector stores only `selectedPathId`; its cards, visible credit equation, semester nodes, and explanation-graph focus all derive from the exact selected candidate. The former credit-comparison/progress fetches, client-side strategy reconstruction, index binding, third graph request, and unexplained weights were removed. Automated UI coverage switches 1 -> 2 -> 3 -> 1, proves that only the selected candidate's course node remains, and asserts exactly one Degree Path HTTP request.

**Mock Registration product audit and decision:** P6 Mock Registration has unique historical value as a student-owned, non-binding future-course intent with immutable revisions, withdrawal, validation, replay, and privacy-preserving demand support. That value is distinct from official Registration but overlaps/confuses the primary student planning experience. The standalone link is therefore hidden from both normal student navigation implementations, while the authenticated direct route, policy-context deep link, API client, P6 backend, replay/history, tests, and sandbox compatibility remain intact. Decision: `HIDE`, not delete or merge in this slice.

**All-17 curriculum audit:** the 16 owner-supplied release JSON artifacts were independently recalculated from their requirement groups; every current canonical total equals its group-required-credit sum. Membership totals exceed degree totals where groups contain elective alternatives and are not used as the degree requirement. AI is the separately existing canonical 132-credit plan. The previously completed fresh-student all-17 engine run reconciled placed plus valid blocked/unplaced credits to each current canonical total; the new validator makes this accounting an enforced per-candidate invariant. Current results are: AI 132/132/132 PASS; SE 132/132/132 PASS; CS 132/132/132 PASS; CYBER 132/132/132 PASS; MATH 129/129/129 PASS; PHYS 129/129/129 PASS; ARABIC 132/132/132 PASS; ENGLISH 129/129/129 PASS; HISTORY 129/129/129 PASS; TRANS 129/129/129 PASS; ECON 129/129/129 PASS; BBA 129/129/129 PASS; ACC 129/129/129 PASS; DJOURN 132/132/132 PASS; RTV 132/132/132 PASS; LAW 141/141/141 PASS; NURS 129/129/129 INTERNAL PASS / AUTHORITATIVE FAIL. Values are `canonical/group sum/Degree Path accounting`.

**Nursing detailed evidence:** current `ZU:NURS:NURS:P12` has six groups requiring `18 + 9 + 21 + 66 + 3 + 12 = 129`, 63 memberships totaling 168 raw credits because elective alternatives coexist, and current Degree Path accounting of 129 (103 placed plus 26 valid blocked/unplaced in the fresh-student audit). The official Zarqa University page publishes those same six requirements plus a distinct 3-credit free-elective requirement, so the official degree requirement is 132. `MISSING_OR_INCORRECT_ITEM = omitted 3-credit free-elective requirement group`; `EXPECTED_CREDITS = 132`; `CURRENT_CREDITS = 129`. No specific course or membership is asserted or invented. Exact reviewed correction must add the missing governed free-elective requirement and its authoritative selection semantics at the curriculum source/generator, regenerate canonical artifacts, update the Fake University and Morshidi seeds/migration through local database validation, and allow every consuming engine to derive 132 naturally. Production mutation is explicitly stopped pending owner review.

Production Next build: PASS (40 static/dynamic routes generated). Repository-wide ESLint: FAIL only on two pre-existing `react/no-unescaped-entities` errors at unchanged `apps/web/src/app/institutional/pilots/[pilot_id]/page.tsx:840`. Final full backend: **2350 passed, 15 failed, 102 skipped, 6 warnings in 122.07s**; failures are the established unrelated four Advisor-explanation expectations and eleven CORS/settings cases, and local-Supabase tests are environment-skipped. `git diff --check` passes with informational LF-to-CRLF notices only. No auth, ownership, registration implementation, curriculum artifact, migration, database, Supabase resource, or production resource changed; the existing Degree Path route response contract was extended only with the same-result explanation graph.

## Degree Path deterministic bounded-search root-cause fix (2026-10-21, implementation checkpoint)

**Objective/slice:** eliminate the production Degree Path failure generically for all 17 curricula without changing authentication, ownership, routes, academic data, or fail-closed prerequisite semantics. Starting branch/SHA: `main` / `ffbaf30e3f1ff7affcf91ca206d62855d4554c42`; starting worktree was clean.

**Production and timeout evidence:** public Render health returned 200 and the live Vercel bundle contains the prior 120-second browser transport limit. The API retains a 50-second computation budget; catalog HTTP operations use the shared httpx client's 5-second per-operation default; repository catalog reads are batched rather than per-course; route/service/serialization timings already exist. Authenticated Morshidi production reproduction and Render logs remain unavailable because this checkout/account has neither the synthetic-student passwords nor access to Morshidi Supabase project `lzwttbjnuhdllesfuzzs` or Render. No credential reset, brute force, academic-data mutation, or production write occurred.

**Root cause and implementation:** exact canonical Nursing P12 fresh-student input took 11.56 seconds CPU before the change; 11.27 seconds was semester planning, with 179,340 subsets materialized and fully simulated. Phase 8 exhaustively enumerated up to `2^15` subsets for every Degree Path state. Degree Path now invokes a deterministic 256-state semester frontier while standalone semester-planner behavior remains exhaustive by default. The frontier rejects any selected course that does not advance canonical plan credits, preventing surplus elective alternatives. A fail-closed final validator recomputes every semester and verifies prerequisite order, plan membership, duplicates/completed-course reuse, term load, raw/canonical credits, remaining required courses, terminal status, and typed errors: `DUPLICATE_COURSE`, `FOREIGN_PLAN_COURSE`, `MISSING_PLAN_COURSE`, `CREDIT_TOTAL_MISMATCH`, `PREREQUISITE_ORDER_VIOLATION`, `UNRESOLVED_DEPENDENCY`, `COMPLETED_COURSE_REPLACED`, `INVALID_TERM_LOAD`, and `RESULT_MISMATCH`. Validation duration is included in existing phase telemetry.

**17-plan evidence:** canonical fresh-student fixtures for all 17 plans completed in 0.604–1.677 seconds; P50 1.260 seconds, P95/MAX 1.677 seconds; canonical input load was 0.6–1.5 ms and validation 0–16 ms at Windows clock resolution. Every plan reconciled `placed canonical credits + valid blocked/unplaced credits = canonical plan total`. Nursing: 63 memberships, total 129, placed 103, blocked/unplaced 26, 1.083 seconds in the final all-plan run. Each result stopped only at authoritative review-required rules and no unresolved rule was guessed. Measured times fit the existing synchronous 50-second service lifecycle with wide margin, so no async job or cache was added.

Final full backend: **2348 passed, 15 failed, 102 skipped**. The exact same 15 failures (4 advisor-explanation and 11 CORS) reproduced at the clean starting SHA in a temporary detached worktree (**28 passed, 15 failed**), proving no new regression; the skips require unavailable Local Supabase variables.
